# 前端加密的分析与突破-先知社区

> **来源**: https://xz.aliyun.com/news/19247  
> **文章ID**: 19247

---

# 前言

在现如今的互联网生态中，前端技术早已超越了简单的页面展示与用户交互，演变为一场复杂而精密的“攻防博弈”。随着数据价值的日益凸显，各大平台为了保护自身核心资源、防止自动化爬虫和恶意请求，纷纷在前端布下层层防护：从动态生成的加密参数、时间戳签名，到复杂的代码混淆（obfuscation）、环境校验乃至行为分析，安全策略不断升级，手段愈发隐蔽。面对这些精心设计的障碍，前端逆向工程成为理解系统逻辑、实现合法数据交互不可或缺的技术能力。

本文将围绕一个真实的前端对抗案例展开，通过剖析登录认证、接口签名、动态加密参数等典型场景。我们将从浏览器调试入手，定位关键加密函数，还原混淆代码，到扣js代码补齐加密参数最终利用 Python 结合 JS 环境实现自动化调用，完成数据的高效获取。

# 案例一图片.png

![图片.png](images/20251111160748-8341673e-bed5-1.png)

题目的意思就是要把20页里面的current\_array数组里面的值全部加起来，但是get请求里面有一个sign参数是不断变化的，就需要逆向出sign的加密逻辑，再用脚本爬取current\_array数组进行相加

找加密位置的3种方法：

方法一：既然知道了加密参数，那么找加密地点最快的方法就是直接全局搜索js文件里面含有sign参数的位置

![图片.png](images/20251111160749-836dba1e-bed5-1.png)

直接在这里下一个断点看看是不是加密后的位置

![图片.png](images/20251111160749-838cdb42-bed5-1.png)

可以发现确实是

![图片.png](images/20251111160749-83a5f4c6-bed5-1.png)

方法二：xhr打断点，找一个标志性的特征，一般选择url后的路由

![图片.png](images/20251111160749-83bfe776-bed5-1.png)

点击加号添加

![图片.png](images/20251111160749-83d127ca-bed5-1.png)

刷新界面即可断住

![图片.png](images/20251111160749-83f0b324-bed5-1.png)

方法三：启动器入手，从加载页面的这个js入手

![图片.png](images/20251111160750-840cea76-bed5-1.png)

点击即可跳转到这儿

![图片.png](images/20251111160750-8423c462-bed5-1.png)

打上断点，刷新界面，即可发现加密位置

那么我们就可以从js代码中获取这些信息

```
sign=window.token
window.token = window.md5("tuling" + timestamp + pageNumber)  //MD5加密，tuling为盐值
const timestamp = new Date().getTime()
pageNumber    //参数为页数
```

加密所需要的参数及加密方式已经确定了

接下来可以扣js代码了,大致的逻辑就是这样了，接下来看看还缺什么参数接着扣

```
function get_sign(pageNumber) {
    const timestamp = new Date().getTime()
    const sign = window.md5("tuling" + timestamp + pageNumber)
    console.log(sign)
}
```

![图片.png](images/20251111160750-843d32ee-bed5-1.png)

MD5加密在我们本地肯定是没有的，但是它的js代码对MD5加密进行了重写，那我们只需要把window方法也给扣下来即可

![图片.png](images/20251111160750-8452641e-bed5-1.png)

function get_sign(pageNumber) {
    const timestamp = new Date().getTime()
    const sign = window.md5("tuling" + timestamp + pageNumber)
    console.log(sign)
}

get_sign(1)

![图片.png](images/20251111160750-847494f0-bed5-1.png)

可以看到已经可以生成sign了，接下来写一个python脚本去发包即可,复制bash，放入[爬虫工具库](https://spidertools.cn/#/curl2Request)一键生成爬虫代码

![图片.png](images/20251111160750-84923534-bed5-1.png)

![图片.png](images/20251111160751-84cc1a9a-bed5-1.png)

接下来再修改其中参数即可

这里要python代码与js代码进行交互需要安装pyexecjs库

```
import requests
import execjs

# 读取并编译 JS 文件
with open("1.js", "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    js_code = f.read()

ctx = execjs.compile(js_code)

# 请求头
headers = {
    "accept": "*/*",
    "accept-language": "zh-CN,zh;q=0.9",
    "priority": "u=1, i",
    "referer": "https://mashangpa.com/problem-detail/4/",
    "sec-ch-ua": '"Not?A_Brand";v="99", "Chromium";v="130"',
    "sec-ch-ua-mobile": "?0",
    "sec-ch-ua-platform": '"Windows"',
    "sec-fetch-dest": "empty",
    "sec-fetch-mode": "cors",
    "sec-fetch-site": "same-origin",
    "user-agent": "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/130.0.6723.70 Safari/537.36"
}

# Cookie（请确保有效）
cookies = {
    "sessionid": "",
    "Hm_lvt_0d2227abf9548feda3b9cb6fddee26c0": "1761821851,1761822906,1761878480",
    "HMACCOUNT": "FB751129E2D5993E",
    "Hm_lpvt_0d2227abf9548feda3b9cb6fddee26c0": "1761880377"
}

# 存储总和
total_sum = 0

# API 地址
url = "https://mashangpa.com/api/problem-detail/4/data/"

# 遍历 page 1 到 20
for page in range(1, 21):
    try:
        # 调用 JS 函数生成 sign 和 timestamp
        result = ctx.call("get_sign", page)
        sign = result["sign"]
        timestamp = result["ts"]

        # 请求参数
        params = {
            "page": str(page),
            "sign": sign,
            "_ts": timestamp
        }

        # 发起 GET 请求
        response = requests.get(url, headers=headers, cookies=cookies, params=params)
        response.raise_for_status()  # 检查 HTTP 错误

        data = response.json()

        if "current_array" in data:
            current_sum = sum(data["current_array"])
            total_sum += current_sum
            print(f"Page {page}: {data['current_array']} -> Sum = {current_sum}")
        else:
            print(f"Page {page}: 'current_array' not found in response.")

    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error on page {page}: {e}")

# 输出最终结果
print(f"
所有数组元素总和: {total_sum}")
```

# 案例二

![图片.png](images/20251111160751-8528fc62-bed5-1.png)

查看请求就可以发现这里页多了一个加密的xl参数

![图片.png](images/20251111160752-8553e11e-bed5-1.png)

全局搜索一下该参数

![图片.png](images/20251111160752-857819da-bed5-1.png)

![图片.png](images/20251111160752-859089d4-bed5-1.png)

接下来看一下代码所需要的参数

```
xl: encryptedQuery
let encryptedQuery = encrypt(jsonString);
const jsonString = JSON.stringify(params);
const params = {
        page: pageNumber,
        _ts: timestamp,
    };
```

接下来就可以大概写一下加密逻辑

```
function demo(pageNumber) {
    const timestamp = new Date().getTime();
    const params = {
        page: pageNumber,
        _ts: timestamp
    };
    const xl = encrypt(JSON.stringify(params));
    return xl;
}
```

就只差encrypt方法不知道了

看看encrypt方法

![图片.png](images/20251111160752-85a80046-bed5-1.png)

可以发现这里进行了一个ob混淆

ob解混淆方法1，直接把鼠标放参数上面

​

![图片.png](images/20251111160752-85bd577a-bed5-1.png)

方法2：控制台解密

![图片.png](images/20251111160753-85ce6c90-bed5-1.png)

方法三：直接在线网站：<https://tool.yuanrenxue.cn/decode_obfuscator>

这里页声明了encrypt方法里面的参数

![图片.png](images/20251111160753-85e6e50c-bed5-1.png)

解混淆后：

```
const CryptoJS = require("crypto-js");

function encrypt(dataString) {
    const key = CryptoJS.enc.Utf8.parse('jo8j9wGw%6Hbxffn');
    const iv = CryptoJS.enc.Utf8.parse('0123456789ABCDEF');

    const encrypted = CryptoJS.AES.encrypt(dataString, key, {
        iv: iv,
        mode: CryptoJS.mode.CBC,
        padding: CryptoJS.pad.Pkcs7
    });

    return encrypted.ciphertext.toString(CryptoJS.enc.hex);
}
```

所有可以写出完整js代码了

```
const CryptoJS = require("crypto-js");

function encrypt(dataString) {
    const key = CryptoJS.enc.Utf8.parse('jo8j9wGw%6Hbxffn');
    const iv = CryptoJS.enc.Utf8.parse('0123456789ABCDEF');

    const encrypted = CryptoJS.AES.encrypt(dataString, key, {
        iv: iv,
        mode: CryptoJS.mode.CBC,
        padding: CryptoJS.pad.Pkcs7
    });

    return encrypted.ciphertext.toString(CryptoJS.enc.hex);
}
function demo(pageNumber) {
    const timestamp = new Date().getTime();
    const params = {
        page: pageNumber,
        _ts: timestamp
    };
    const xl = encrypt(JSON.stringify(params));
    return xl;
}
demo(1)
```

这里的加密和第一个一样的，都是自己写的一个方法，直接把window方法扣下来

```
function xxxxoooo(encryptedHex) {
    let enccc = dd.a.enc.Hex.parse(encryptedHex);
    let deccc = dd.a.AES["decr" + "ypt"]({ciphertext: enccc}, kkkk, {
        mode: dd.a.mode.CBC,
        padding: dd.a.pad.Pkcs7,
        iv: iiii,
    });
    return deccc.toString(dd.a.enc.Utf8);
}
```

function xxxxoooo(encryptedHex) {
    let kkkk = CryptoJS.enc.Utf8.parse("xxxxxxxxoooooooo");
    let iiii = CryptoJS.enc.Utf8.parse("0123456789ABCDEF");
    let enccc = CryptoJS.enc.Hex.parse(encryptedHex);
    let deccc = CryptoJS.AES["decrypt"]({ciphertext: enccc}, kkkk, {
        mode: CryptoJS.mode.CBC,

import json
import execjs
import requests

# 需要登录，请先登录，获取cookie并完善参数
cookies = {
    "sessionid": "vms1bj0csqnb07ismwl8xb5r2peh3f0q",
    "Hm_lvt_0d2227abf9548feda3b9cb6fddee26c0": "1761822906,1761878480,1761881876,1761884653",
    "HMACCOUNT": "FB751129E2D5993E",
    "Hm_lpvt_0d2227abf9548feda3b9cb6fddee26c0": "1761884662"
}

var = 0
for i in range(1, 21):
    with open('1.js', 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        js_code = f.read()
    result = execjs.compile(js_code).call('demo')
    print(result)
    s = result['s']
    tt = str(result['ttt'])
    headers = {
        "accept": "*/*",
        "accept-language": "zh-CN,zh;q=0.9",
        "priority": "u=1, i",
        "referer": "https://mashangpa.com/problem-detail/6/",
        "s": s,
        "sec-ch-ua": ""Not?A_Brand";v="99", "Chromium";v="130"",
        "sec-ch-ua-mobile": "?0",
        "sec-ch-ua-platform": ""Windows"",
        "sec-fetch-dest": "empty",
        "sec-fetch-mode": "cors",
        "sec-fetch-site": "same-origin",
        "tt": tt,
        "user-agent": "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/130.0.6723.70 Safari/537.36"
    }
    params = {
        'page': i,
    }
    res = requests.get('https://www.mashangpa.com/api/problem-detail/6/data/', headers=headers, params=params,
                       cookies=cookies)
    data = res.json()['t']
    val = execjs.compile(js_code).call('xxxxoooo', data)
    # print(val)
    val_json = json.loads(val)
    print(val_json)
    var += sum(val_json['current_array'])
print(f"和为：{var}")

![图片.png](images/20251111160758-890d2c28-bed5-1.png)

接下来寻找解密代码

![图片.png](images/20251111160758-89268c62-bed5-1.png)

和上面一样的思路，扣出来，哪里缺了补哪里

```
let kkkk = CryptoJS["enc"]["Utf8"]["parse"]("xxxxxxxxoooooooo"),
    iiii = CryptoJS["enc"]["Utf8"]["parse"]("0123456789ABCDEF");

    "ciphertext": _0x3b2b73

    "padding": CryptoJS["pad"]["Pkcs7"],
    "iv": iiii
  });

  return _0x198675["toString"](CryptoJS["enc"]["Utf8"]);
}
```

# 实战案例

**AES 加解密简介**

高级加密标准(AES,Advanced Encryption Standard)为最常见的对称加密算法（微信小程序）加密传输就是用这个加密算法的)。对称加密算法也就是加密和解密用相同的密钥，具体的加密流程如下图：

![](images/20260326223120-753a121a-2920-1.png)

这里很明显就可以发现请求和响应都被加密了

![图片.png](images/20251111160759-896fb8e2-bed5-1.png)

全局搜索加密函数encrypt

![图片.png](images/20251111160759-89ad33b4-bed5-1.png)

动调后可以获取以下信息

```
加密算法: AES
模式: CBC
填充: PKCS7
密钥: eHwvBmaIKWkTYyMBDKEHqQ== 
初始化向量(IV): e33e6370750c37d9
```

使用解密工具解密

![图片.png](images/20251111160759-89e7bd4a-bed5-1.png)

遍历参数值即可获取敏感信息

![图片.png](images/20251111160800-8a2a8fee-bed5-1.png)

一键生成爬虫代码

![图片.png](images/20251111160750-84923534-bed5-1.png)

![图片.png](images/20251111160751-84cc1a9a-bed5-1.png)

接下来再修改其中参数即可

这里要python代码与js代码进行交互需要安装pyexecjs库

```
import requests
import execjs

# 读取并编译 JS 文件
with open("1.js", "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    js_code = f.read()

ctx = execjs.compile(js_code)

# 请求头
headers = {
    "accept": "*/*",
    "accept-language": "zh-CN,zh;q=0.9",
    "priority": "u=1, i",
    "referer": "https://mashangpa.com/problem-detail/4/",
    "sec-ch-ua": '"Not?A_Brand";v="99", "Chromium";v="130"',
    "sec-ch-ua-mobile": "?0",
    "sec-ch-ua-platform": '"Windows"',
    "sec-fetch-dest": "empty",
    "sec-fetch-mode": "cors",
    "sec-fetch-site": "same-origin",
    "user-agent": "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/130.0.6723.70 Safari/537.36"
}

# Cookie（请确保有效）
cookies = {
    "sessionid": "",
    "Hm_lvt_0d2227abf9548feda3b9cb6fddee26c0": "1761821851,1761822906,1761878480",
    "HMACCOUNT": "FB751129E2D5993E",
    "Hm_lpvt_0d2227abf9548feda3b9cb6fddee26c0": "1761880377"
}

# 存储总和
total_sum = 0

# API 地址
url = "https://mashangpa.com/api/problem-detail/4/data/"

# 遍历 page 1 到 20
for page in range(1, 21):
    try:
        # 调用 JS 函数生成 sign 和 timestamp
        result = ctx.call("get_sign", page)
        sign = result["sign"]
        timestamp = result["ts"]

        # 请求参数
        params = {
            "page": str(page),
            "sign": sign,
            "_ts": timestamp
        }

        # 发起 GET 请求
        response = requests.get(url, headers=headers, cookies=cookies, params=params)
        response.raise_for_status()  # 检查 HTTP 错误

        data = response.json()

        if "current_array" in data:
            current_sum = sum(data["current_array"])
            total_sum += current_sum
            print(f"Page {page}: {data['current_array']} -> Sum = {current_sum}")
        else:
            print(f"Page {page}: 'current_array' not found in response.")

    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error on page {page}: {e}")

# 输出最终结果
print(f"
所有数组元素总和: {total_sum}")
```

```
xl: encryptedQuery
let encryptedQuery = encrypt(jsonString);
const jsonString = JSON.stringify(params);
const params = {
        page: pageNumber,
        _ts: timestamp,
    };
```

接下来就可以大概写一下加密逻辑

```
function demo(pageNumber) {
    const timestamp = new Date().getTime();
    const params = {
        page: pageNumber,
        _ts: timestamp
    };
    const xl = encrypt(JSON.stringify(params));
    return xl;
}
```

就只差encrypt方法不知道了

看看encrypt方法

![图片.png](images/20251111160752-85a80046-bed5-1.png)

可以发现这里进行了一个ob混淆

ob解混淆方法1，直接把鼠标放参数上面

​

![图片.png](images/20251111160752-85bd577a-bed5-1.png)

方法2：控制台解密

![图片.png](images/20251111160753-85ce6c90-bed5-1.png)

方法三：直接在线网站：<https://tool.yuanrenxue.cn/decode_obfuscator>

这里页声明了encrypt方法里面的参数

![图片.png](images/20251111160753-85e6e50c-bed5-1.png)

解混淆后：

```
const CryptoJS = require("crypto-js");

function encrypt(dataString) {
    const key = CryptoJS.enc.Utf8.parse('jo8j9wGw%6Hbxffn');
    const iv = CryptoJS.enc.Utf8.parse('0123456789ABCDEF');

    const encrypted = CryptoJS.AES.encrypt(dataString, key, {
        iv: iv,
        mode: CryptoJS.mode.CBC,
        padding: CryptoJS.pad.Pkcs7
    });

    return encrypted.ciphertext.toString(CryptoJS.enc.hex);
}
```

所有可以写出完整js代码了

```
const CryptoJS = require("crypto-js");

function encrypt(dataString) {
    const key = CryptoJS.enc.Utf8.parse('jo8j9wGw%6Hbxffn');
    const iv = CryptoJS.enc.Utf8.parse('0123456789ABCDEF');

    const encrypted = CryptoJS.AES.encrypt(dataString, key, {
        iv: iv,
        mode: CryptoJS.mode.CBC,
        padding: CryptoJS.pad.Pkcs7
    });

    return encrypted.ciphertext.toString(CryptoJS.enc.hex);
}
function demo(pageNumber) {
    const timestamp = new Date().getTime();
    const params = {
        page: pageNumber,
        _ts: timestamp
    };
    const xl = encrypt(JSON.stringify(params));
    return xl;
}
demo(1)
```

请求的问题已经解决了，接下来该解决响应的问题了

跟进一下可以发现存在解密逻辑

![图片.png](images/20251111160755-8761d538-bed5-1.png)

```
function xxxxoooo(encryptedHex) {
    let enccc = dd.a.enc.Hex.parse(encryptedHex);
    let deccc = dd.a.AES["decr" + "ypt"]({ciphertext: enccc}, kkkk, {
        mode: dd.a.mode.CBC,
        padding: dd.a.pad.Pkcs7,
        iv: iiii,
    });
    return deccc.toString(dd.a.enc.Utf8);
}
```

function xxxxoooo(encryptedHex) {

    let enccc = CryptoJS.enc.Hex.parse(encryptedHex);
    let deccc = CryptoJS.AES["decrypt"]({ciphertext: enccc}, kkkk, {

```

python代码：

```
import json
import execjs
import requests

# 需要登录，请先登录，获取cookie并完善参数
cookies = {
    "sessionid": "vms1bj0csqnb07ismwl8xb5r2peh3f0q",
    "Hm_lvt_0d2227abf9548feda3b9cb6fddee26c0": "1761822906,1761878480,1761881876,1761884653",
    "HMACCOUNT": "FB751129E2D5993E",
    "Hm_lpvt_0d2227abf9548feda3b9cb6fddee26c0": "1761884662"
}

var = 0
for i in range(1, 21):
    with open('1.js', 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        js_code = f.read()
    result = execjs.compile(js_code).call('demo')
    print(result)
    s = result['s']
    tt = str(result['ttt'])
    headers = {
        "accept": "*/*",
        "accept-language": "zh-CN,zh;q=0.9",
        "priority": "u=1, i",
        "referer": "https://mashangpa.com/problem-detail/6/",
        "s": s,
        "sec-ch-ua": ""Not?A_Brand";v="99", "Chromium";v="130"",
        "sec-ch-ua-mobile": "?0",
        "sec-ch-ua-platform": ""Windows"",
        "sec-fetch-dest": "empty",
        "sec-fetch-mode": "cors",
        "sec-fetch-site": "same-origin",
        "tt": tt,
        "user-agent": "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/130.0.6723.70 Safari/537.36"
    }
    params = {
        'page': i,
    }
    res = requests.get('https://www.mashangpa.com/api/problem-detail/6/data/', headers=headers, params=params,
                       cookies=cookies)
    data = res.json()['t']
    val = execjs.compile(js_code).call('xxxxoooo', data)
    # print(val)
    val_json = json.loads(val)
    print(val_json)
    var += sum(val_json['current_array'])
print(f"和为：{var}")

![图片.png](images/20251111160757-88abb13e-bed5-1.png)

![图片.png](images/20251111160758-88ec2192-bed5-1.png)

# 实战案例

**AES 加解密简介**

高级加密标准(AES,Advanced Encryption Standard)为最常见的对称加密算法（微信小程序）加密传输就是用这个加密算法的)。对称加密算法也就是加密和解密用相同的密钥，具体的加密流程如下图：

![](images/20260326223120-753a121a-2920-1.png)

这里很明显就可以发现请求和响应都被加密了

![图片.png](images/20251111160759-896fb8e2-bed5-1.png)

全局搜索加密函数encrypt

![图片.png](images/20251111160759-89ad33b4-bed5-1.png)

动调后可以获取以下信息

```
加密算法: AES
模式: CBC
填充: PKCS7
密钥: eHwvBmaIKWkTYyMBDKEHqQ== 
初始化向量(IV): e33e6370750c37d9
```

使用解密工具解密

![图片.png](images/20251111160759-89e7bd4a-bed5-1.png)

遍历参数值即可获取敏感信息

![图片.png](images/20251111160800-8a2a8fee-bed5-1.png)

# 一键生成爬虫代码

![图片.png](images/20251111160750-84923534-bed5-1.png)

![图片.png](images/20251111160751-84cc1a9a-bed5-1.png)

接下来再修改其中参数即可

这里要python代码与js代码进行交互需要安装pyexecjs库

```
import requests
import execjs

# 读取并编译 JS 文件
with open("1.js", "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    js_code = f.read()

ctx = execjs.compile(js_code)

# 请求头
headers = {
    "accept": "*/*",
    "accept-language": "zh-CN,zh;q=0.9",
    "priority": "u=1, i",
    "referer": "https://mashangpa.com/problem-detail/4/",
    "sec-ch-ua": '"Not?A_Brand";v="99", "Chromium";v="130"',
    "sec-ch-ua-mobile": "?0",
    "sec-ch-ua-platform": '"Windows"',
    "sec-fetch-dest": "empty",
    "sec-fetch-mode": "cors",
    "sec-fetch-site": "same-origin",
    "user-agent": "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/130.0.6723.70 Safari/537.36"
}

# Cookie（请确保有效）
cookies = {
    "sessionid": "",
    "Hm_lvt_0d2227abf9548feda3b9cb6fddee26c0": "1761821851,1761822906,1761878480",
    "HMACCOUNT": "FB751129E2D5993E",
    "Hm_lpvt_0d2227abf9548feda3b9cb6fddee26c0": "1761880377"
}

# 存储总和
total_sum = 0

# API 地址
url = "https://mashangpa.com/api/problem-detail/4/data/"

# 遍历 page 1 到 20
for page in range(1, 21):
    try:
        # 调用 JS 函数生成 sign 和 timestamp
        result = ctx.call("get_sign", page)
        sign = result["sign"]
        timestamp = result["ts"]

        # 请求参数
        params = {
            "page": str(page),
            "sign": sign,
            "_ts": timestamp
        }

        # 发起 GET 请求
        response = requests.get(url, headers=headers, cookies=cookies, params=params)
        response.raise_for_status()  # 检查 HTTP 错误

        data = response.json()

        if "current_array" in data:
            current_sum = sum(data["current_array"])
            total_sum += current_sum
            print(f"Page {page}: {data['current_array']} -> Sum = {current_sum}")
        else:
            print(f"Page {page}: 'current_array' not found in response.")

    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error on page {page}: {e}")

# 输出最终结果
print(f"
所有数组元素总和: {total_sum}")
```

# 案例二

![图片.png](images/20251111160751-8528fc62-bed5-1.png)

查看请求就可以发现这里页多了一个加密的xl参数

![图片.png](images/20251111160752-8553e11e-bed5-1.png)

全局搜索一下该参数

![图片.png](images/20251111160752-857819da-bed5-1.png)

![图片.png](images/20251111160752-859089d4-bed5-1.png)

接下来看一下代码所需要的参数

```
xl: encryptedQuery
let encryptedQuery = encrypt(jsonString);
const jsonString = JSON.stringify(params);
const params = {
        page: pageNumber,
        _ts: timestamp,
    };
```

接下来就可以大概写一下加密逻辑

```
function demo(pageNumber) {
    const timestamp = new Date().getTime();
    const params = {
        page: pageNumber,
        _ts: timestamp
    };
    const xl = encrypt(JSON.stringify(params));
    return xl;
}
```

就只差encrypt方法不知道了

看看encrypt方法

![图片.png](images/20251111160752-85a80046-bed5-1.png)

可以发现这里进行了一个ob混淆

ob解混淆方法1，直接把鼠标放参数上面

​

![图片.png](images/20251111160752-85bd577a-bed5-1.png)

方法2：控制台解密

![图片.png](images/20251111160753-85ce6c90-bed5-1.png)

方法三：直接在线网站：<https://tool.yuanrenxue.cn/decode_obfuscator>

这里页声明了encrypt方法里面的参数

![图片.png](images/20251111160753-85e6e50c-bed5-1.png)

解混淆后：

```
const CryptoJS = require("crypto-js");

function encrypt(dataString) {
    const key = CryptoJS.enc.Utf8.parse('jo8j9wGw%6Hbxffn');
    const iv = CryptoJS.enc.Utf8.parse('0123456789ABCDEF');

    const encrypted = CryptoJS.AES.encrypt(dataString, key, {
        iv: iv,
        mode: CryptoJS.mode.CBC,
        padding: CryptoJS.pad.Pkcs7
    });

    return encrypted.ciphertext.toString(CryptoJS.enc.hex);
}
```

所有可以写出完整js代码了

```
const CryptoJS = require("crypto-js");

function encrypt(dataString) {
    const key = CryptoJS.enc.Utf8.parse('jo8j9wGw%6Hbxffn');
    const iv = CryptoJS.enc.Utf8.parse('0123456789ABCDEF');

    const encrypted = CryptoJS.AES.encrypt(dataString, key, {
        iv: iv,
        mode: CryptoJS.mode.CBC,
        padding: CryptoJS.pad.Pkcs7
    });

    return encrypted.ciphertext.toString(CryptoJS.enc.hex);
}
function demo(pageNumber) {
    const timestamp = new Date().getTime();
    const params = {
        page: pageNumber,
        _ts: timestamp
    };
    const xl = encrypt(JSON.stringify(params));
    return xl;
}
demo(1)
```

# 案例三

![图片.png](images/20251111160754-866082f6-bed5-1.png)

这一关可以发现是请求响应被加密了

![图片.png](images/20251111160754-867f9ba8-bed5-1.png)

![图片.png](images/20251111160754-869ec5fa-bed5-1.png)

所以这里无法进行全局搜索来定位加密，这里需要从启动器入手

![图片.png](images/20251111160754-86ba1136-bed5-1.png)

可以发现这里发送请求的时候携带了hearder信息

![图片.png](images/20251111160754-86d43c46-bed5-1.png)

打个断点调一下，发现确实为加密数据，接下来需要去找hhh

![图片.png](images/20251111160754-86ecac7a-bed5-1.png)

这里写清楚了参数构造

![图片.png](images/20251111160755-86fd9e06-bed5-1.png)

再定位一下xxo

![图片.png](images/20251111160755-87107ce2-bed5-1.png)

这里的加密和第一个一样的，都是自己写的一个方法，直接把window方法扣下来

```
function xxxxoooo(encryptedHex) {
    let enccc = dd.a.enc.Hex.parse(encryptedHex);
    let deccc = dd.a.AES["decr" + "ypt"]({ciphertext: enccc}, kkkk, {
        mode: dd.a.mode.CBC,
        padding: dd.a.pad.Pkcs7,
        iv: iiii,
    });
    return deccc.toString(dd.a.enc.Utf8);
}
```

function xxxxoooo(encryptedHex) {
    let kkkk = CryptoJS.enc.Utf8.parse("xxxxxxxxoooooooo");
    let iiii = CryptoJS.enc.Utf8.parse("0123456789ABCDEF");
    let enccc = CryptoJS.enc.Hex.parse(encryptedHex);
    let deccc = CryptoJS.AES["decrypt"]({ciphertext: enccc}, kkkk, {
        mode: CryptoJS.mode.CBC,
        padding: CryptoJS.pad.Pkcs7,
        iv: iiii,

```
import json
import execjs
import requests

# 需要登录，请先登录，获取cookie并完善参数
cookies = {
    "sessionid": "vms1bj0csqnb07ismwl8xb5r2peh3f0q",
    "Hm_lvt_0d2227abf9548feda3b9cb6fddee26c0": "1761822906,1761878480,1761881876,1761884653",
    "HMACCOUNT": "FB751129E2D5993E",
    "Hm_lpvt_0d2227abf9548feda3b9cb6fddee26c0": "1761884662"
}

var = 0
for i in range(1, 21):
    with open('1.js', 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        js_code = f.read()
    result = execjs.compile(js_code).call('demo')
    print(result)
    s = result['s']
    tt = str(result['ttt'])
    headers = {
        "accept": "*/*",
        "accept-language": "zh-CN,zh;q=0.9",
        "priority": "u=1, i",
        "referer": "https://mashangpa.com/problem-detail/6/",
        "s": s,
        "sec-ch-ua": ""Not?A_Brand";v="99", "Chromium";v="130"",
        "sec-ch-ua-mobile": "?0",
        "sec-ch-ua-platform": ""Windows"",
        "sec-fetch-dest": "empty",
        "sec-fetch-mode": "cors",
        "sec-fetch-site": "same-origin",
        "tt": tt,
        "user-agent": "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/130.0.6723.70 Safari/537.36"
    }
    params = {
        'page': i,
    }
    res = requests.get('https://www.mashangpa.com/api/problem-detail/6/data/', headers=headers, params=params,
                       cookies=cookies)
    data = res.json()['t']
    val = execjs.compile(js_code).call('xxxxoooo', data)
    # print(val)
    val_json = json.loads(val)
    print(val_json)
    var += sum(val_json['current_array'])
print(f"和为：{var}")

![图片.png](images/20251111160757-88abb13e-bed5-1.png)

  'x': x}
}
console.log(get_data())
```

![图片.png](images/20251111160758-890d2c28-bed5-1.png)

接下来寻找解密代码

![图片.png](images/20251111160758-89268c62-bed5-1.png)

和上面一样的思路，扣出来，哪里缺了补哪里

```
let kkkk = CryptoJS["enc"]["Utf8"]["parse"]("xxxxxxxxoooooooo"),
    iiii = CryptoJS["enc"]["Utf8"]["parse"]("0123456789ABCDEF");

function xxxxoooo(_0x5477b4) {
  let _0x3b2b73 = CryptoJS["enc"]["Hex"]["parse"](_0x5477b4),
      _0x198675 = CryptoJS["AES"]["decrypt"]({
    "ciphertext": _0x3b2b73
  }, kkkk, {
    "mode": CryptoJS["mode"]["CBC"],
    "padding": CryptoJS["pad"]["Pkcs7"],
    "iv": iiii
  });

  return _0x198675["toString"](CryptoJS["enc"]["Utf8"]);
}
```

# 实战案例

**AES 加解密简介**

高级加密标准(AES,Advanced Encryption Standard)为最常见的对称加密算法（微信小程序）加密传输就是用这个加密算法的)。对称加密算法也就是加密和解密用相同的密钥，具体的加密流程如下图：

![](images/20260326223120-753a121a-2920-1.png)

这里很明显就可以发现请求和响应都被加密了

![图片.png](images/20251111160759-896fb8e2-bed5-1.png)

全局搜索加密函数encrypt

![图片.png](images/20251111160759-89ad33b4-bed5-1.png)

动调后可以获取以下信息

```
加密算法: AES
模式: CBC
填充: PKCS7
密钥: eHwvBmaIKWkTYyMBDKEHqQ== 
初始化向量(IV): e33e6370750c37d9
```

使用解密工具解密

![图片.png](images/20251111160759-89e7bd4a-bed5-1.png)

遍历参数值即可获取敏感信息

![图片.png](images/20251111160800-8a2a8fee-bed5-1.png)
